Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Item 8.01 Other Events.</span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">As previously disclosed, on July 17, 2025, the parties to the stockholder derivative action in the Delaware Court of Chancery, captioned </span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">In re Facebook Inc. Derivative Litigation</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">, Consolidated C.A. 2018-0307-KSJM (Del. Ch.) (the "Matter"), agreed to a settlement in principle to resolve all claims in the Matter, which is subject to court approval.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">On December 5, 2025, the Court of Chancery entered a Scheduling Order pursuant to which a settlement hearing will be held at 1:30 pm ET on April 7, 2026. The Scheduling Order requires that the parties' Stipulation and Agreement of Settlement, Compromise, and Release (the "Stipulation") and the Notice of Pendency of the Matter, Proposed Settlement of the Matter, and Settlement Hearing (the "Notice") be distributed to stockholders of Meta Platforms, Inc. (the "Company") in the form of this Current Report on Form 8-K. The Stipulation is attached hereto as Exhibit 99.1 and the Notice is attached hereto as Exhibit 99.2.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div legal merit, but also believe that the Settlement set forth below provides substantial and immediate benefits for Meta and its stockholders.  In addition to these substantial benefits, Co-Lead Plaintiffs and Co-Lead Counsel have considered&#58; (i)&#160;the attendant risks of continued litigation and the uncertainty of the outcome of the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims&#59; (ii)&#160;the probability of success on the merits of the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims&#59; (iii)&#160;problems of proof associated with, and possible defenses to, the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims&#59; (iv) the probability of recovering equitable relief and&#47;or damages at trial&#59; (v)&#160;the desirability of permitting the Settlement to be consummated according to its terms&#59; (vi)&#160;the expense and length of continued proceedings necessary to prosecute the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims against Defendants through trial and</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">14</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">appeals&#59; and (vii)&#160;the conclusion of Co-Lead Plaintiffs and Co-Lead Counsel that the terms and conditions of the Stipulation are fair, reasonable, and adequate, and that it is in the best interests of Meta and Meta&#8217;s stockholders to settle the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims on the terms set forth herein.</font></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">EEE.&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;Based on Co-Lead Counsel&#8217;s extensive review and analysis of the relevant facts, allegations, defenses, and controlling legal principles, Co-Lead Counsel believe that the Settlement set forth in this Stipulation is fair, reasonable, and adequate, and confers substantial benefits upon Meta and its stockholders.  Based upon Co-Lead Counsel&#8217;s and Co-Lead Plaintiffs&#8217; evaluation, Co-Lead Plaintiffs have determined that the Settlement is in the best interests of Meta and its stockholders and have agreed to the terms and conditions set forth herein.</font></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">FFF.&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;Defendants deny any and all allegations of fault, liability, wrongdoing, or damages whatsoever alleged in this Action, and make no admission of liability or any form of wrongdoing whatsoever.  Without limiting the generality of the foregoing, Defendants further deny that they engaged in any wrongdoing or committed any violation of law or breach of duty, or aided and abetted any such breach or wrongdoing, or have violated any law or statutory duty whatsoever,  and Defendants</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">15</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">expressly maintain that at all relevant times they acted properly, in good faith, and diligently and scrupulously complied with their statutory, fiduciary, and other legal duties.  Defendants are entering into the Settlement and this Stipulation solely to avoid the substantial burden, expense, inconvenience, and distraction of continued litigation and to finally and forever put to rest, resolve, and terminate the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims.  The Settlement and this Stipulation shall in no event be construed as, or deemed to be, evidence of or an admission or concession on the part of any of the Defendants with respect to any claim or factual allegation or of any fault or liability or wrongdoing or damage whatsoever or any infirmity in the defenses that any of the Defendants have or could have asserted.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">NOW, THEREFORE, IT IS HEREBY STIPULATED, CONSENTED TO, AND AGREED, by and among the Parties through their attorneys of record and subject to approval of the Court, that, pursuant to Court of Chancery Rule 23.1 and the other conditions set forth herein, for the good and valuable consideration set forth herein to be conferred on Meta and its stockholders, the Action shall be fully and finally settled, compromised, and dismissed on the merits with prejudice as to the Defendants, upon and subject to the terms and conditions of this Stipulation.  The Released Claims shall be fully, finally, and forever released, relinquished, settled, </font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">16</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">and discharged with prejudice as to the Released Parties in the manner and upon the terms and conditions set forth herein.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:36pt;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#010000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">I.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%;padding-left:29.61pt">Definitions</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">In addition to the terms defined elsewhere in this Stipulation, the following capitalized terms used in this Stipulation and any Exhibits attached hereto shall have the meanings specified below&#58;</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:72pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">(a)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:24.91pt">&#8220;Business Day&#8221; With respect to any and all Released Claims, the Parties stipulate and agree that, upon the Effective Date, Plaintiffs and Defendants shall expressly waive, and each Current Meta Stockholder and Released Party shall be deemed to have waived, and by operation of the Judgment shall have expressly waived, relinquished, and released any and all provisions, rights, and benefits conferred by any law of any state or territory of the United States or other jurisdiction, or principle of common law or foreign law, which is similar, comparable, or equivalent to Cal. Civ. Code &#167;1542, which provides&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:36pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">A GENERAL RELEASE DOES NOT EXTEND TO CLAIMS THAT THE CREDITOR OR RELEASING PARTY DOES NOT KNOW OR SUSPECT TO EXIST IN HIS OR HER FAVOR AT THE TIME OF EXECUTING THE RELEASE AND THAT, IF KNOWN BY HIM OR HER, WOULD HAVE MATERIALLY AFFECTED HIS OR HER SETTLEMENT WITH THE DEBTOR OR RELEASED PARTY.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">The Released Parties acknowledge, and each Current Meta Stockholder by operation of law is deemed to acknowledge, that they may discover facts in addition to or different from those now known or believed to be true with respect to the Released Claims, but that it is the intention of the Parties, and by operation of law, the Released Parties and each Current Meta Stockholder, to completely, fully, finally, and forever extinguish any and all Released Claims, whether known claims or Unknown Claims, suspected or unsuspected, which now exist, or heretofore existed, or may hereafter exist, and without regard to the subsequent discovery of additional or different facts, that could have been asserted in a derivative capacity.  The Parties</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">25</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">also acknowledge, and the Released Parties and each Current Meta Stockholder by operation of law are deemed to acknowledge, that the inclusion of &#8220;Unknown Claims&#8221; in the definition of Released Claims is separately bargained for and is a key element of the Settlement.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:36pt;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#010000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">II.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%;padding-left:25.72pt">Settlement Consideration</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">1.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Monetary Settlement Amount</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">.  If, within three (3) business days after the Settlement Hearing, the Court enters an order approving both the Settlement and the Fee and Expense Award, then Defendants shall cause the Insurers to&#58; (i) pay the Net Settlement Amount to Meta within two (2) Business Days after the Court&#8217;s approval of the Settlement and receiving all information reasonably required to process that payment&#59; and (ii) pay the Fee and Expense Award to an account controlled by Co-Lead Counsel within two (2) business days after the Court&#8217;s approval of the Fee and Expense Award and receiving all information reasonably required to process that payment. </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">2.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">If the Court enters an order approving the Settlement within three (3) Business Days after the Settlement Hearing but has not yet issued an order concerning the Fee and Expense Application, then Defendants shall cause the Insurers to&#58; (i) pay to Meta the Monetary Settlement Amount, less the maximum fees and expenses sought by Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel within two (2) Business Days after the Court&#8217;s approval of the Settlement and receiving all information reasonably required</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">26</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">to process that payment&#59; and (ii) pay the maximum fees and expenses sought by Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel into the Escrow Account on the fourth (4</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;position:relative;top:-3.5pt;vertical-align:baseline">th</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">) Business Day following the Settlement Hearing.  No later than two (2) Business Days after the Court enters an order approving the Fee and Expense Award, Co-Lead Counsel shall transfer the Fee and Expense Award to an account they control, together with any proportional interest earned while on deposit, and transfer any remaining funds in the Escrow Account to Meta.  </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">3.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">If the Court has not entered an order approving the Settlement within three (3) Business Days of the Settlement Hearing, then Defendants shall cause the Insurers to pay the Monetary Settlement Amount into the Escrow Account on the fourth (4</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;position:relative;top:-3.5pt;vertical-align:baseline">th</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">) Business Day following the Settlement Hearing.  If, after the Insurers&#8217; payment into the Escrow Account, the Court enters an order approving the Settlement and any Fee and Expense Award on the same date, then, Co-Lead Counsel shall transfer from the Escrow Account&#58; (i) the Net Settlement Amount to Meta, together with any proportional interest earned while on deposit, no later than two (2) Business Days after the Court&#8217;s approval of the Settlement and receiving all information reasonably required to process that payment&#59; and (ii) the Fee and Expense Award to an account controlled by Co-Lead Counsel, together with any proportional interest earned while on deposit, no later than two (2) Business Days after the Court enters an order approving the Fee and Expense Award and receiving</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">27</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">all information reasonably required to process payment.  If, after the Insurers&#8217; payment into the Escrow Account, the Court enters an order approving the Settlement but reserves decision on the Fee and Expense Application, then Co-Lead Counsel shall&#58; (i) transfer to Meta the Monetary Settlement Amount, together with any proportional interest earned while on deposit, less the maximum fees and expenses sought by Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel, no later than two (2) Business Days after the Court&#8217;s approval of the Settlement and receiving all information reasonably required to process that payment&#59; and (ii) transfer the Fee and Expense Award to an account Co-Lead Plaintiffs control, together with any proportional interest earned while on deposit, and transfer any remaining funds in the Escrow Account to Meta, no later than two (2) Business Days after the Court enters an order approving the Fee and Expense Award.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">4.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">All funds held by the Escrow Agent shall be deemed to be in the custody of the Court and shall remain subject to the jurisdiction of the Court until the funds are distributed or returned in accordance with the Stipulation and&#47;or any further order of the Court.  The Escrow Agent shall invest all funds in the Escrow Account exclusively in United States Treasury Bills (or a mutual fund that invests solely in such instruments) and shall collect and reinvest all interest earned.  However, any residual cash balances up to the amount insured by the FDIC may be deposited in an account that is fully insured by the FDIC.  If the yield on United States Treasury</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">28</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">Bills is negative, the Escrow Agent may, in lieu of purchasing such Treasury Bills, deposit all or any portion of the funds in an account that is fully insured by the FDIC or backed by the faith and credit of the United States.  Additionally, if short-term placement of the funds is necessary, all or any portion of the funds may be deposited in such account.  The Released Defendant Parties shall have no responsibility for, interest in, or liability of any kind relating to the Escrow Agent&#8217;s investment decisions or actions.    Co-lead Counsel shall ensure that any Taxes owed are paid from the Settlement Fund on a timely basis, without further order of the Court. Any tax returns prepared for the Settlement Fund (and any elections made therein) shall comply with this Stipulation and shall state that all Taxes on the income earned by the Settlement Fund shall be paid from the Settlement Fund as provided herein. The Released Defendants Parties shall have no responsibility or liability for any act or omission of Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel or their agents in connection with the payment of such Taxes.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">5.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">If the Settlement is not approved, or is otherwise terminated or cancelled under this Stipulation, the Insurers&#8217; payment of the Monetary Settlement Amount, together with any interest earned while in the Escrow Account, shall be refunded.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">6.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Corporate Governance Measures</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;text-decoration:underline">.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">  Within ninety (90) Business Days after the Effective Date, Meta will undertake the corporate governance measures</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">29</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">described in Exhibit A to this Stipulation (the &#8220;Corporate Governance Measures&#8221;).  The Parties agree that the Corporate Governance Measures confer substantial benefits on Meta and its stockholders, and that Plaintiffs&#8217; efforts caused, or otherwise substantially and materially contributed to, those measures, which Plaintiffs represent were consistent with Plaintiffs&#8217; goals in filing this Action.  Meta further agrees that this Action was a significant factor in the Company appointing multiple new independent directors to the Board since April 2018.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:36pt;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#010000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">III.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%;padding-left:21.83pt">The Releases and Scope of Settlement</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">7.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">The obligations incurred under this Stipulation are in consideration of&#58; (i) the full and final disposition of the Action&#59; and (ii) the Releases provided for under this Stipulation.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">8.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Pursuant to the Judgment, without further action by anyone, upon the Effective Date of the Settlement, the Action shall be dismissed with prejudice, on the merits, and without costs.  The Parties shall bear their own fees, costs, and expenses except as expressly provided </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">in this Stipulation. The Fee and Expense Award shall be payable solely from the Monetary Settlement Amount.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">26.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:23.5pt">Resolution of the Fee and Expense Award is not a condition to the Settlement, entry of the Judgment, or the dismissal with prejudice of the Defendants</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">37</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">in this Action.  The Court may consider and rule upon the fairness, reasonableness, and adequacy of the Settlement independent of any award of attorneys&#8217; fees and expenses.  Any disapproval or modification of any Fee and Expense Application by the Court or on appeal shall not affect or delay the enforceability of this Stipulation, including the effectiveness of the releases set forth in paragraphs 3-6 of this Stipulation, provide any of the Parties with the right to terminate the Settlement, impose any obligation on any of the Defendants or Meta, or subject them in any way to an increase in the amount paid by them or on their behalf in connection with the Settlement, or affect or delay the binding effect or finality of the Judgment and the release of the Released Claims. </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">27.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:23.5pt">In the event that the Fee and Expense Award is disapproved, reduced, reversed, or otherwise modified, as a result of any further proceedings, including any successful collateral attack, then Co-Lead Counsel shall remit to Meta the amount of any portion of the Fee and Expense Award that Co-Lead Counsel has received but to which Co-Lead Counsel is no longer entitled no later than twenty (20) Business Days following the date that the order vacating or reducing the amount of the Fee and Expense Award becomes final and no longer subject to further appeal or review.  In the event that the amount of any Fee and Expense Award is increased as a result of further judicial review or appeal of the Fee and Expense Award and the Monetary Settlement Amount has already been paid, Meta shall remit to Co-Lead</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">38</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">Counsel the amount of such increase no later than twenty (20) Business Days after the date that the order increasing the amount of any Fee and Expense Award becomes final and no longer subject to further appeal or review.  For the avoidance of doubt, no Court order or reversal on appeal of any order concerning the Fee and Expense Award shall operate to terminate or cancel this Stipulation and&#47;or the Settlement, or constitute grounds for termination or cancellation of this Stipulation and&#47;or the Settlement.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">28.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:23.5pt">Co-Lead Counsel warrants that no portion of any Fee and Expense Award shall be paid to any Plaintiff, except as approved by the Court.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">29.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:23.5pt">The Released Defendant Parties shall have no input into, or responsibility or liability for, the allocation by Co-Lead Counsel of the Fee and Expense Award.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:36pt;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#010000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">IX.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%;padding-left:22.39pt">Termination of Settlement&#59; Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.45pt;padding-left:10.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Whistleblower Program </font></div><div style="margin-top:8pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:18pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta&#8217;s whistleblower program prohibits retaliation against employees for reporting alleged privacy violations of law or regulations, including any &#8220;known and suspected violations of (a) laws and governmental rules and regulations, (b) accounting, internal accounting controls, and auditing matters, or (c) Meta&#8217;s Code of Conduct or other policies.  This includes formal or informal information, complaints, or reports received from persons inside or outside of Meta.&#8221;</font></div><div style="margin-top:8.2pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:10.8pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta employees have the ability to make reports regarding any suspected compliance violations by phone, email, website, and other means, including through the SpeakUp hotline, and any such reports can be made anonymously.</font></div><div style="margin-top:8.05pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:14.4pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta will incorporate into its existing whistleblower program more explicit language regarding reporting concerns about privacy violations of law and regulations, including violations of the 2020 FTC Consent Order or its provisions or Meta&#8217;s privacy policies.</font></div><div style="margin-top:8.2pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:25.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta will report summaries of all identified privacy issues raised through whistleblower reports through the SpeakUp hotline to a designated committee of the Board on a quarterly basis.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;padding-left:18pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.45pt">2.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.45pt;padding-left:10.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Director Code of Conduct </font></div><div style="margin-top:8.2pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:25.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta will adopt a separate director code of conduct that will include provisions covering conflicts of interest, confidentiality, compliance with laws and regulations, and illegal behavior.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;padding-left:18pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.55pt">3.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.55pt;padding-left:10.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Director Conflict of Interest Policy </font></div><div style="margin-top:7.75pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:10.8pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">Meta will amend its Director Conflicts of Interest Policy (last amended September 7, 2022, and attached) to remove Mark Zuckerberg&#8217;s decision-making authority over potential director &#8220;conflicts&#8221; as follows&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;padding-left:54pt;padding-right:10.8pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">o</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:13pt;text-decoration:underline">Assessment of Potential Conflicts</font></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;padding-right:3.6pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">If a Director believes that he or she, directly or through an affiliated organization, is likely to become involved in a business relationship that could create a Potential Conflict, the Director will notify Meta&#8217;s Chief Legal Officer (CLO) or the designee of the CLO of such proposed relationship prior to engaging in the Potential Conflict.  If the CLO or the CLO&#8217;s designee determines that the proposed relationship is a Potential Conflict, the Potential Conflict will be evaluated by the Lead Independent Director to assess whether any conflict would thereby be created, provided that in the event of a Potential Conflict involving the</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="padding-left:72pt;padding-right:3.6pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">Lead Independent Director, the matter will be evaluated the chairperson of the Compensation, Nominating and Governance Committee (&#8220;CNGC&#8221;) (any such assessment, an &#8220;Assessment&#8221;).</font></div><div style="margin-top:13.6pt;padding-left:72pt;padding-right:3.6pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">In the event of any disagreement among the parties conducting the Assessment, the CNGC shall make the final determination.  In addition, any Potential Conflicts may be escalated to the CNGC for final determination at the discretion of the Lead Independent Director or the chairperson of CNGC, or at the request of the director subject to the evaluation of a Potential Conflict, as applicable.  To the extent the CNGC may evaluate, discuss, or assess conflicts, no member of the CNGC involved in such Potential Conflict shall participate.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;padding-left:18pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.55pt">4.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:12.55pt;padding-left:10.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Insider Trading Policy</font></div><div style="margin-top:7.8pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">All 10b5-1 trading plans under Meta&#8217;s 10b5-1 Trading Plan Policy for executive officers shall be subject to the approval or ratification of the CLO or CLO&#8217;s designee before any stock sales contemplated by such plans may be effectuated, and any such trading plan may only be terminated early or modified (as defined in Meta&#8217;s 10b5-1 Trading Plan Policy) once per year.</font></div><div style="margin-top:8.25pt;padding-left:36pt;padding-right:10.8pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:13.65pt;padding-left:14.5pt">When deciding whether to approve a 10b5-1 plan, the CLO or CLO&#8217;s designee shall consider whether the executive officer may be in possession of any material nonpublic information and any certification provided concerning the same.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New (i) within seven (7) calendar days hereof, Meta shall file a copy of this Stipulation and the Notice, substantially in the form attached hereto as Exhibit B, as exhibits to a Form 8-K with the United States Securities and Exchange Commission, and (ii) within seven (7) calendar days hereof, Meta shall publish the Stipulation and the Notice, substantially in the form of Exhibit C to the Stipulation, within the investor relations section of Meta&#8217;s website, landing page available at https&#58;&#47;&#47;investor.atmeta.com.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">8.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Meta shall bear the Notice Costs, and in no event shall Defendants, Plaintiffs, any Current Meta Stockholder, or Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel proof of mailing and publication of the Notice.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">10.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:23.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Appearance at Settlement Hearing and Objections</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;  This Order and Final Judgment incorporates and makes a part hereof the Stipulation and all of its terms, conditions, provisions, and exhibits.  All terms herein with initial capitalization that are not defined in this Order shall have the meanings ascribed to them in the Stipulation.</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">D-1</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">2.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Jurisdiction</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;   The Court has jurisdiction over the subject matter of the Action, and all matters relating to the Settlement and Meta stockholders, as well as personal jurisdiction over the Parties for purposes of the Settlement.  It is further determined that Plaintiffs, Defendants, Meta, and all Meta stockholders, as well as their heirs, executors, successors, and assigns, are bound by this Judgment.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">3.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Notice</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;  The Court finds that the dissemination of the Notice&#58;  (a) was implemented in accordance with the Scheduling Order&#59; (b)&#160;constituted the best notice reasonably practicable under the circumstances&#59; (c)&#160;constituted due, adequate, and sufficient notice to all Persons entitled to receive notice of the Settlement&#59; and (d)&#160;met the requirements of Court of Chancery Rule 23.1, due process, and applicable law.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">4.</font><font Claims</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;  The Settlement of this Action set forth in the Stipulation is fully and finally approved as fair, reasonable, and adequate, and in the best interests of Meta.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">5.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Pursuant to Court of Chancery Rule 23.1, this Court approves the terms and conditions of the Settlement set forth in the Stipulation in all respects (including, without limitation&#58;  the releases, including the release of the Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims as against the Released Defendant Parties and the release of the Released Defendants&#8217; Claims as against the Released Plaintiff Parties&#59; and the dismissal with prejudice of the claims asserted against Defendants in the Action), and finds that the</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">D-2</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">Settlement is, in all respects, fair, reasonable, and adequate to Meta, and the Parties are hereby authorized and directed to consummate the Settlement in accordance with the terms and provisions of the Stipulation, and the Register of Chancery is directed to enter and docket this Order and Final Judgment.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">6.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">The Action and all of the claims asserted against Defendants in the Action by Plaintiffs are hereby dismissed with prejudice, on the merits and without costs, except as otherwise expressly provided in the Stipulation and this Judgment.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">7.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;  The releases set forth in paragraphs 3-6 of the Stipulation (the &#8220;Releases&#8221;) are expressly incorporated herein in all respects.  The Releases are effective as of the Effective Date.  Accordingly, this Court orders that, as of the Effective Date and without further action by anyone&#58;</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:72pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">a.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:29.07pt">The Action is dismissed with prejudice, on the merits, and without costs.  The Parties shall bear their own fees, costs, and expenses except as expressly provided in the Stipulation.  As of the Effective Date, the Released Parties shall be deemed to be released and forever discharged from all of the Released Claims&#59; </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:72pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">b.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Meta acting directly, Plaintiffs or any other Company stockholder acting derivatively on behalf of Meta, and any of their respective legal representatives, heirs, executors, administrators, predecessors, successors, predecessors-in-interest, successors-in interest and assigns, shall be deemed to have,</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">D-3</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">and by operation of law and of the Judgment shall have, fully, finally, completely, and forever compromised, settled, released, resolved, relinquished, waived, discharged, extinguished, and dismissed with prejudice, and shall forever be barred and enjoined from commencing, instituting, instigating, facilitating, asserting, maintaining, participating in, supporting, or prosecuting any and all Released Plaintiffs&#8217; Claims (including Unknown Claims) against any of the Defendants and the other Released Defendant Parties&#59; </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:72pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">c.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:29.07pt">Plaintiffs, on behalf of themselves only, agree and covenant not to authorize or solicit the commencement or prosecution against any of the Released Defendant Parties of any action or proceeding asserting direct claims related to or arising from the allegations that were asserted or could have been asserted in the Action&#59; provided, however, that this Order and Judgment shall not extinguish or limit Plaintiffs&#8217; rights to participate in any settlement or award obtained in connection with any action asserting direct claims commenced or prosecuted by any party or parties other than themselves&#59; and </font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:72pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">d.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Defendants and Meta shall be deemed to have, and by operation of law and the Judgment shall have, completely, fully, finally, and forever, compromised, settled, released, discharged, extinguished, relinquished, and dismissed with prejudice, and shall forever be enjoined from commencing, instituting, instigating, facilitating, asserting, maintaining, participating in, or</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">D-4</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">prosecuting any and all Released Defendants&#8217; Claims against the Released Plaintiff Parties.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">8.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt">Notwithstanding the above paragraph, nothing in the Stipulation or in this Order and Final Judgment shall bar any action by any of the Parties to enforce the terms of the Stipulation or this Order and Final Judgment.  Nothing in the Stipulation or this Order and Final Judgment including the above paragraph, shall affect or waive Defendants&#8217; claims for advancement or indemnity of their legal fees, costs, and expenses incurred in connection with the Action and the Settlement, or any claims or rights that any Defendant may have against any of their respective insurers, coinsurers, or reinsurers.  Nothing in the Stipulation or this Order and Final Judgment, including the above paragraph, constitutes or reflects a waiver or release of any rights or claims that Defendants or Meta may have regarding the confidentiality of materials provided to Plaintiffs or Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel, including the right for Defendants or Meta to seek to enforce the terms of any confidentiality agreements or protective orders that Plaintiffs or Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel entered into in connection with their receipt of confidential materials from Defendants or Meta.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">9.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:232%;padding-left:28.5pt;text-decoration:underline">Fee and Expense Award</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">&#58;  Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel are hereby awarded a Fee and Expense Award consisting of attorney&#8217;s fees in the sum of $____________ and expenses in the sum of $_____, which the Court finds to be fair and reasonable.  Co-Lead Plaintiffs are each hereby awarded a Service Award of $____________.</font></div><div style="height:72pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div considered separate from this Judgment.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New costs, and expenses.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New this Order and Final Judgment.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Derivative Litigation</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">, C.A. No. 2018-0307-KSJM (the &#8220;Action&#8221;), which was brought by plaintiffs California State Teachers&#8217; Retirement System (&#8220;CalSTRS&#8221;), Construction and General Building Laborers&#8217; Local Union No. 79 General Fund (&#8220;Local 79&#8221;), and City of Birmingham Retirement and Relief System (&#8220;Birmingham&#8221;) (collectively, &#8220;Co-Lead Plaintiffs&#8221;), and plaintiffs Firemen&#8217;s Retirement System of St. Louis (&#8220;St. Louis&#8221;), Lidia Levy (&#8220;Levy&#8221;), and Karen Sbriglio (&#8220;Sbriglio&#8221;) (together with Co-Lead Plaintiffs, the &#8220;Plaintiffs&#8221;), on behalf of and for the benefit of the Company, in the Court of Chancery of the State of Delaware (the &#8220;Court&#8221;)&#59; (ii) a proposed settlement of the Action (the &#8220;Settlement&#8221;), subject to the approval of the Court, as provided in the Stipulation and Agreement of Settlement, Compromise, and Release dated November 20, 2025 (the &#8220;Settlement Stipulation&#8221;)&#59; (iii)&#160;the hearing that the Court will hold on April 7, 2026, at 1&#58;30 p.m. ET, to determine whether to approve the proposed Settlement and to consider the application by Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel for an award of attorneys&#8217; fees and litigation expenses, including any incentive awards to Plaintiffs to be deducted solely from any fee and expense award to Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel&#59; and (iv)&#160;Current Company Stockholders&#8217; rights with respect to the proposed Settlement and the application for attorneys&#8217; fees and expenses.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;position:relative;top:-3.5pt;vertical-align:baseline">1</font></div><div style="border-bottom:1pt solid #000000;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-top:10pt;opacity:1;width:150pt"></div><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;position:relative;top:-3.5pt;vertical-align:baseline">1</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%"> All capitalized terms not otherwise defined in this Notice shall have the meaning provided in the Settlement Stipulation or the Scheduling Order, which are available in the &#8220;Investor Relations&#8221; section of the Company&#8217;s website, https&#58;&#47;&#47;investor.atmeta.com&#47;Legal-Notices&#47;default.aspx.</font></div><div style="height:63pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:31.5pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">PLEASE READ THIS NOTICE CAREFULLY AND IN ITS ENTIRETY.<br>YOUR RIGHTS WILL BE AFFECTED BY THE PROPOSED</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">SETTLEMENT OF THIS LAWSUIT.  </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The Settlement Stipulation was entered into as of November 20, 2025, by and among the following parties&#58; (i)&#160;Plaintiffs, derivatively on behalf of the Company&#59; (ii)&#160;defendants Mark Zuckerberg, Sheryl K. Sandberg, Konstantinos Papamiltiadis, Jeffrey D. Zients, Peggy Alford, Kenneth I. Chenault, Peter A. Thiel, Susan D. Desmond-Hellmann, Reed Hastings, Marc L. Andreessen, and Erskine B. Bowles (the &#8220;Individual Defendants&#8221;) and (iii)&#160;nominal defendant Meta (together with the Individual Defendants, &#8220;Defendants&#8221; and together with Co-Lead Plaintiffs and Defendants, the &#8220;Parties,&#8221; and each a &#8220;Party&#8221;), subject to the approval of the Court.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">As described in paragraph 55 below, the Settlement provides for a cash payment of $190,000,000.00 (the &#8220;Settlement Amount&#8221;), which, after deducting any Court-awarded attorneys&#8217; fee and expenses and any applicable taxes, will be paid to the Company, and the implementation of corporate governance reforms.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Because the Action was brought as a derivative action, which means that the Action was brought by Plaintiffs on behalf of and for the benefit of the Company, the cash recovery from the Settlement will go to the Company.  Individual Meta stockholders will not receive any direct payment from the Settlement.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%;text-decoration:underline">PLEASE NOTE</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">&#58; THERE IS NO PROOF OF CLAIM FORM FOR STOCKHOLDERS TO SUBMIT IN CONNECTION WITH THIS SETTLEMENT, AND STOCKHOLDERS ARE NOT REQUIRED TO TAKE ANY ACTION IN RESPONSE TO THIS NOTICE.</font></div><div style="padding-left:5.4pt"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:100.000%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"></td><td style="width:98.900%"></td><td style="width:0.1%"></td></tr><tr><td colspan="3" style="background-color:#e6e6e6;border-bottom:0.5pt solid #000000;border-left:0.5pt solid #000000;border-right:0.5pt solid #000000;border-top:0.5pt solid #000000;padding:2px 1pt;text-align:left;vertical-align:top"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;margin-top:6pt;padding-left:19.32pt;padding-right:19.32pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">WHAT IS THE PURPOSE OF THIS NOTICE&#63; </font></div></td></tr></table></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">1.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">The purpose of this Notice is to explain the Action, the terms of the proposed Settlement, and how the proposed Settlement affects Company stockholders&#8217; legal rights.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">2.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">In a derivative action, one or more persons or entities who are current stockholders of a corporation sue on behalf of and for the benefit of the corporation, seeking to enforce the corporation&#8217;s legal rights.  In this case, Plaintiffs have filed</font></div><div style="height:63pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">C-2</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:31.5pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">suit against the Individual Defendants on behalf of and for the benefit of the Company.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">3.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">The Court has scheduled a hearing to consider the fairness, reasonableness, and adequacy of the Settlement and the application by Plaintiffs&#8217; Counsel for an award of attorneys&#8217; fees and expenses (the &#8220;Settlement Hearing&#8221;).  See paragraphs 64-72 below for details about the Settlement Hearing, including the location, date, and time of the hearing.</font></div><div style="padding-left:5.4pt"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:100.000%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"></td><td style="width:98.900%"></td><td style="width:0.1%"></td></tr><tr><td colspan="3" style="background-color:#e6e6e6;border-bottom:0.5pt solid #000000;border-left:0.5pt solid #000000;border-right:0.5pt solid #000000;border-top:0.5pt solid #000000;padding:2px 1pt;text-align:left;vertical-align:top"><div style="margin-bottom:6pt;margin-top:6pt;padding-left:19.32pt;padding-right:19.32pt;text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">WHAT IS THIS CASE ABOUT&#63;  WHAT HAS HAPPENED <br>SO FAR&#63;</font></div></td></tr></table></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;margin-top:12pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">THE FOLLOWING DESCRIPTION OF THE ACTION AND THE SETTLEMENT HAS BEEN PREPARED BY COUNSEL FOR THE PARTIES.  THE COURT HAS MADE NO FINDINGS WITH RESPECT TO SUCH MATTERS, AND THIS NOTICE IS NOT AN EXPRESSION OR STATEMENT BY THE COURT OF FINDINGS OF FACT.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">A MORE COMPLETE STATEMENT OF THE FACTS OF THIS MATTER IS SET FORTH IN THE PARTIES&#8217; PLEADINGS AND BRIEFING. ATTACHED HERETO AS EXHIBITS A AND B ARE THE PRE-TRIAL BRIEFS FILED IN THIS ACTION, WHICH SUMMARIZE THE PARTIES&#8217; CLAIMS AND DEFENSES. PLEASE SEE PARAGRAPH 73 BELOW FOR MORE INFORMATION ABOUT HOW AND WHERE TO LOCATE THOSE DOCUMENTS.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">1.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">In this action, Plaintiffs assert claims for breaches of fiduciary duty against the Individual Defendants, and a claim for trading on the basis of the Company&#8217;s material non-public information against Defendant Mark Zuckerberg.  </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">2.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">The claims in this Action stem from the Individual Defendants&#8217; alleged failure to ensure Facebook complied with the July 27, 2012, Federal Trade Commission (the &#8220;FTC&#8221;) final consent order against the Company (the &#8220;2012 Consent Order&#8221;) that finalized a settlement with Facebook resolving the FTC&#8217;s allegations in FTC Docket No. C-4365 relating to the Company&#8217;s privacy practices and privacy disclosures, as well as the approval in 2019 by Defendants Zuckerberg, Sandberg, Andreessen, Chenault, Desmond-Hellmann, Thiel, and Zients of a $5 billion payment to settle the FTC&#8217;s claim that Facebook had violated the 2012 Consent Order (the &#8220;2019 Settlement&#8221;).</font></div><div style="height:63pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">C-3</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:31.5pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">3.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">The Individual Defendants deny all of Plaintiffs&#8217; allegations and deny that they engaged in any wrongdoing or committed any violation of law or breach of fiduciary duties and believe that at all relevant times they acted properly, in good faith, and in a manner consistent with their legal duties.  Specifically, the Individual Defendants deny that they caused Meta to violate the 2012 Consent Order and deny that they failed to provide proper oversight to ensure that Meta did not violate the 2012 Consent Order. </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%"> The Individual Defendants further deny they breached their fiduciary duties in approving the 2019 Settlement.  Defendant Zuckerberg denies that he sold any stock on the basis of material, non-public information.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">4.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:28.5pt">A more complete recitation of the claims, defenses and issues in this Action is contained in Exhibits A and B, the pre-trial briefs the parties filed in this Action.   </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:12pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New FTC&#8217;s allegations in FTC Docket No. C-4365 related to the Company&#8217;s privacy practices and privacy disclosures.  </font></div><div
